# User questions and answers

Every question any mdpro session puts to the user, with the user's answer quoted
verbatim. Answers given in a session's chat get buried under its peer messages;
this file is where they are kept. The rule that governs it is in `CLAUDE.md`
§ "User questions and answers" (user decision, 2026-10-14:
「目前我在本session和各个session直接问的问题的回答会被很多消息淹没掉，之后你单独维护一个md文档」).

- **Newest entry first.** Entries are append-only: a later answer that changes an
  earlier one is a new entry that names the one it replaces. An old entry is
  never edited.
- **One entry per question.** Several questions asked in one prompt each get
  their own entry.
- **Quote the answer exactly** (the option label, plus any note the user typed).
- **Say where it was acted on**: the ADR, sequencing-doc row, rule or commit
  that carries the decision, or "—" when it was a one-off permission.

## Backfill from earlier sessions (2026-09-09 to 2026-10-14 08:35)

Generated on 2026-10-14 by an echo-sleuth recall subagent, at the user's request (「你调用 /echo-sleuth:recall 将本project各个session中我提的问题，以及回复，补充到上面文档中，用一个subagent完成」). It covers 68 session transcripts across the mdpro project dirs: 691 AskUserQuestion asks and 89 prose questions paired with the user's next reply, marked "(plain text)". Rows the user declined say "(declined the question)".

How far to trust each column:
- **Answer** is verbatim.
- **Question** is cut to its first line, about 260 characters.
- **Asked by** shows `unnamed (<session id>)` when the session never set a name.
- **Item** ids come from pattern matching and may include false hits.
- **Recorded in** is a lead, not proof: it lists files the session edited before the user's next message.

Newest first. This block is a one-time import; new entries go in the Entries table above.

| 2026-09-28 10:15 | mdpro-a3 (3239cbc0) | WI-7b, WI-6b, WI-7, WI-8 | (plain text) ...出码 101 那个已知缺陷。 我接受它的 8 处偏离，最值得知道的是一处生产代码改动：sidecar 的 HTTP 连接不再复用。原因是不关连接池的话，服务器重启后旧连接会在请求中途断开，而这种断开不会触发重试，测试结果就取决于时序。代价是每次请求多建一次本机连接，影响可以忽略。其余偏离是测试放在哪个文件、按矩阵多测的场景，以及额外的加固（拒绝端口 0 和空 token、错误不带系统原文）。 等 WI-7b 做完，我会一次性复跑全量 `cargo test`、改动文件的 clippy、`cargo build --bin mdpro_mcp` 和 sidecar 测试，确认 WI-6b、WI-7、WI-7b、WI-8 这四项。 本会话的第 1 到第 5 步（修 ledger → 对 WI-9 和 WI-10 跑收据 verify → 修 TS6133 → 修过时注释 → 起草 WI-11 文档）还在等你确认。要现在开始吗？ | 现在开始：修 ledger → 对 WI-9 和 WI-10 跑收据 verify → 修 TS6133 → 修过时注释 → 起草 WI-11 文档 | — |
